| [`Stilt.Panics.After`](Stilt/Panics/After.lean) | When the then-branch of an `if` always jumps, ending in `return`, `break`, `continue` or `raise`, the code after the `if` runs only when the condition was false, so it may count on the facts that the condition's negation gives (facts.guarding-tests): `if d == 0 { return 0 }` followed by `n / d`. |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Calls`](Stilt/Panics/Calls.lean) | A call of a panic-free function value, with panic-free arguments, ends as panic-free code may: entering it binds panic-free values, its body is panic-free, a closure's by the value and a declared function's because the value names only functions that `lim` covers (`Decls.PanicCover`), and leaving it writes them back along fields. |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Conds`](Stilt/Panics/Conds.lean) | A condition gives the branches it guards its facts (facts.guarding-tests): a test of a variable against zero reads it and changes nothing, so the branch it takes starts from the same frame, in which the variable holds the integer it compared. |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Facts`](Stilt/Panics/Facts.lean) | When a condition reaches the Boolean `b`, the facts it gives for `b` hold (`eval_facts`): a test's, by how it runs (`eval_zeroTest`); a conjunct's of an `&&` that holds, or a disjunct's of an `||` that doesn't, for the variables that the operands after it don't write (`eval_keeps`); and through `!`. |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Guards`](Stilt/Panics/Guards.lean) | A test gives the code it guards facts about variables (facts.guarding-tests): `d != 0`, `d > 0` or `d < 0` that `d` isn't zero (facts.divisors), `i >= 0` that `i` isn't negative, and `i < xs.len()` that `i` is below the length of the list `xs` holds (facts.guards), each with its mirror, and the negation of a test gives the facts of the opposite test. | [facts.keys](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.keys), [facts.range-loops](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.range-loops), [facts.guarding-tests](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.guarding-tests) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Handling`](Stilt/Panics/Handling.lean) | A `with` whose handler is a panic-free value, a record of closures with panic-free bodies, handles a panic-free block's performances without a panic of its own: each clause it calls is panic-free, on a panic-free argument, from the frame the performance left, and resuming the rest of the block or ending the `with` with the clause's value adds nothing (handlers.with, handlers.resuming and handlers.implicit-effects-and-capabilities). |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Holding`](Stilt/Panics/Holding.lean) | A `when` adds no panic of its own but `deadlock`, which `without panic` doesn't rule out (shared.when-on-value-already-held): holding its cells panics with that kind when one is held, and otherwise takes out of the world panic-free values, which its block's panic-free code works on; putting each name's value back in its cell, however the block ends, keeps every cell's value panic-free (shared.when). | [shared.when-on-value-already-held](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when-on-value-already-held) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Instances`](Stilt/Panics/Instances.lean) | A call instantiates the type parameters of the body it runs, and its row variables (`Stilt.Panics.RowInstances`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Keeps`](Stilt/Panics/Keeps.lean) | Evaluating `e` in copy-in, copy-out, whatever its order of write-back, ends with a frame that has the variables it started with, each with its value unless `e.writes` names it (`eval_keeps`). | [handlers.with](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.with) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Kept`](Stilt/Panics/Kept.lean) | `eval_keeps`: evaluating `e` with any fuel keeps every variable that `e.writes` doesn't name, and the length of every list that `e.resizes` doesn't name, by induction on the fuel, case by case on the expression. |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Keys`](Stilt/Panics/Keys.lean) | `m.contains(k)` that holds shows that the map `m` holds has the key `k`, a variable or a literal, so `m[k]` that follows, while neither is written, finds the key and doesn't panic with `missing-key` (facts.keys). |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Ops`](Stilt/Panics/Ops.lean) | Which of the calculus's operations add the implicit effect `panic` (effects.panicking-operations and effects.panic-kinds): `/` and `%` but by a divisor written as a literal other than zero, `div_euclid` and `rem_euclid`, slicing a string or a list, inserting into a list or removing from it, reading a map's key, and `panic(msg)`, `unreachable()` and the other panics written out. | [std.lists](../reference/17-std.md#std.lists), [std.maps](../reference/17-std.md#std.maps), [effects.panicking-operations](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.panicking-operations) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Overflow`](Stilt/Panics/Overflow.lean) | Which of the calculus's operations add the implicit effect `overflow` (effects.overflow-effect): those that check that an integer fits its type. | [numbers.lengths-are-i64](../reference/06-numbers.md#numbers.lengths-are-i64) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Ranges`](Stilt/Panics/Ranges.lean) | A `for` over a range from an integer literal `a` binds its variable, at each turn, to an integer no less than `a`, so it isn't zero when `a` is positive and isn't negative when `a` isn't (facts.known-signs), and over a range from a variable known not negative, to one that isn't negative either; over `a..xs.len()`, to one below the length of the list `xs` held when the loop started, which it still holds while the body writes no `xs` (facts.range-loops). |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Reaches`](Stilt/Panics/Reaches.lean) | An evaluation reaches a value and a state when it ends with them, or when it performs an operation whose rest, resumed with some value in some world, reaches them (handlers.performing-operation). |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.RowInstances`](Stilt/Panics/RowInstances.lean) | A call instantiates the row variables of the body it runs, as it does its type parameters (`Stilt.Panics.Instances`): the instantiated body writes what the body writes and is panic-free exactly when the body is. |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Runs`](Stilt/Panics/Runs.lean) | The evaluator's steps that run expressions keep `Eval.PanicFree lim`, given that running a panic-free expression does (`PanicFreeRun lim`) and keeps what it doesn't write (`KeepsRun`): evaluating a list of expressions, each guarded by what the ones before it don't write, a record's fields, the indexes of a place without one, a call's arguments, a `match`, an assignment, and a `for` loop, whose turns keep the facts about the variables its body doesn't write. |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Signs`](Stilt/Panics/Signs.lean) | The rules of acceptance know some integer expressions not to be negative (facts.known-signs): a non-negative literal, a call of `len()`, a variable the facts around it know not negative, and a `+` or a `*` of such expressions (`Expr.nonnegIn`). | [facts.known-signs](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.known-signs), [facts.keys](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.keys) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.SignsHold`](Stilt/Panics/SignsHold.lean) | An expression known non-negative that ends with a value ends with an integer that isn't negative (`eval_nonneg`), and the facts that a binding gives its name hold of the frame it binds the name in (`bindFacts_holds`). | [facts.known-signs](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.known-signs) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Steps`](Stilt/Panics/Steps.lean) | `Eval.PanicFree lim G P m`: run on a panic-free state, whose frame and whose world's cells hold panic-free values, and whose variables of `G` hold integers other than zero, `m` ends with a value that `P` holds of, a panic-free raise or jump, each in a panic-free state; with a panic of the kind `deadlock`, which `without panic` doesn't rule out (shared.when-on-value-already-held), or `overflow` where `lim` allows it, which `without panic` alone doesn't rule out (effects.without-overflow); with a performance of a panic-free value, from a panic-free state, whose rest ends so again once resumed with a panic-free value in a world whose cells hold panic-free values; or out of fuel, or stuck. | [handlers.implicit-effects-and-capabilities](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.implicit-effects-and-capabilities), [shared.when](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Stores`](Stilt/Panics/Stores.lean) | `xs[e] = v`, where `e` is a variable `j`, `j + k` or `j - k` that the facts prove an index of `xs` (facts.guards and facts.range-loops), and where `v` doesn't resize `xs`, stores into an element: the index is computed first, then the value, then the store (values.assignment-order), and the list still has the length the index was proved against (`Frame.Keeps.lookup_list`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Syntax`](Stilt/Panics/Syntax.lean) | Whether code holds no operation that adds the implicit effect `panic` (effects.panicking-operations): no index, whether of a place or of a value (std.lists) but one that tests or a range loop prove in bounds, no operation that `Op.panicFree` leaves out, but `/`, `%`, `div_euclid` and `rem_euclid` by a divisor that is proved not zero, and no closure literal whose body holds one (facts.inference), a handler's bodies among them; a performance and a `with` add nothing of their own (handlers.implicit-effects-and-capabilities). | [effects.panicking-operations](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.panicking-operations), [facts.guards](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.guards), [facts.range-loops](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.range-loops), [facts.keys](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.keys), [facts.known-signs](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.known-signs), [facts.divisors](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.divisors), [handlers.implicit-effects-and-capabilities](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.implicit-effects-and-capabilities), [shared.when-on-value-already-held](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when-on-value-already-held), [effects.without-panic](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.without-panic), [facts.calls-through-function-values](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.calls-through-function-values), [effects.without-overflow](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.without-overflow) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Tests`](Stilt/Panics/Tests.lean) | The tests of `Expr.test?` read their variables, and a list's length, and compare them: from a frame, a test that reaches a value leaves the frame as it was, and its value is the comparison of what its variables hold (`eval_test`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Theorem`](Stilt/Panics/Theorem.lean) | **Without panic** (effects.without-panic): code that holds no operation that adds the implicit effect `panic`, divisors that tests guard counted as proved (facts.divisors), and names only functions whose bodies are such code (`Decls.PanicCover`), never panics but with the kinds `overflow` and `deadlock`, which `without panic` doesn't rule out (effects.without-overflow and shared.when-on-value-already-held), and only with `deadlock` when it holds no operation that can overflow either. | [effects.without-panic](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.without-panic), [facts.calls-through-function-values](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.calls-through-function-values), [effects.without-overflow](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.without-overflow) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Values`](Stilt/Panics/Values.lean) | A value is panic-free under `lim` when every closure it holds has a body without an operation that adds `panic`, and every function it names is one that `lim` covers, so that calling it runs such a body, wherever the value was made (facts.calls-through-function-values). | [shared.when](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when) |
| [`Stilt.Panics.Writes`](Stilt/Panics/Writes.lean) | The variables that code may change: those it assigns, and those it passes `inout`, which a call writes back when it ends (values.inout-arguments). |  |
| [`Stilt.Parallel`](Stilt/Parallel.lean) | par.sequential-meaning gives each function of `std.par` the meaning of its sequential run: `map` calls its function on the elements from the first to the last, and `both` calls `f`, then `g`. | [par.sequential-meaning](../reference/14-tasks.md#par.sequential-meaning), [par.panics](../reference/14-tasks.md#par.panics), [values.passing-values](../reference/05-values.md#values.passing-values), [tasks.values-in-tasks](../reference/14-tasks.md#tasks.values-in-tasks) |
| [`Stilt.Parallel.Typed`](Stilt/Parallel/Typed.lean) | A closure of `std.par` has an empty row (par.what-runs-in-parallel), and the fundamental lemma gives a call of a function value of such a type, on arguments of its parameter types, no raise and no performance to end with. | [tasks.continuations-stay-in-task](../reference/14-tasks.md#tasks.continuations-stay-in-task), [tasks.task-functions](../reference/14-tasks.md#tasks.task-functions), [par.sequential-meaning](../reference/14-tasks.md#par.sequential-meaning), [par.panics](../reference/14-tasks.md#par.panics) |

| [`Stilt.Poly.Rigid`](Stilt/Poly/Rigid.lean) | polymorphism.rigid-types checks an argument given where a polymorphic function type `fn<T>(…) -> …` is expected with `T` taken as a rigid type: a type distinct from every other, which only itself is equal to. | [polymorphism.instantiation](../reference/07-types.md#polymorphism.instantiation), [polymorphism.rigid-types](../reference/07-types.md#polymorphism.rigid-types) |

| [`Stilt.Projections.Once`](Stilt/Projections/Once.lean) | `eval_yielding`: a run, with any fuel, of code of a projection's body that passes `Expr.once` yields as its paths do. | [projections.once-on-every-path](../reference/13-modes.md#projections.once-on-every-path) |
| [`Stilt.Projections.Paths`](Stilt/Projections/Paths.lean) | `Outcome.Yielding res N r P o`: on each path through `o`, an outcome of code of a projection's body whose kept values are in `res`, the frame keeps its variables `N` in order, and `res` holds as many values as the path has run `yield`s: the number `r` gives when the path ends normally, with a value that `P` holds of; one when it ends with a `return`; at most one when it raises or reaches a performance, whose rest, resumed with any value in any world, again ends so; and the path ends with no `break` or `continue`. | [projections.once-on-every-path](../reference/13-modes.md#projections.once-on-every-path) |
| [`Stilt.Projections.Runs`](Stilt/Projections/Runs.lean) | Every run of code that passes `Expr.once` yields as its paths do (`eval_yielding`): the statements of a block run in turn, a branch runs one of its arms, a `catch`'s handler runs from what its operand, which runs no `yield`, kept, and a `yield` calls the access once and adds its value to the list kept. |  |
| [`Stilt.Projections.Theorem`](Stilt/Projections/Theorem.lean) | What the checks of projections promise of a run: the body of every projection of an accepted program, run from where its list of kept values is empty, ends every path to its end or to a `return` with one value kept, the access's, and every path to a raise or a performance with at most one (`Program.Projections.yields`, projections.once-on-every-path). | [projections.once-on-every-path](../reference/13-modes.md#projections.once-on-every-path) |
| [`Stilt.Projections.Yield`](Stilt/Projections/Yield.lean) | `yield p` reads the list of kept values, calls the access, which writes no `res` and ends with no jump, and stores the list with the access's value added: from none kept, one (`yield_yielding`, projections.yield and projections.when-projection-runs). | [projections.yield](../reference/13-modes.md#projections.yield) |
| [`Stilt.Quiet.Calls`](Stilt/Quiet/Calls.lean) | A call of a function value whose type's row reads as no `io` runs a body whose own row reads as none: a closure's literal, or a declared function's signature, holds no label the type's row lacks (`FnFits.labels`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Quiet.Cases`](Stilt/Quiet/Cases.lean) | Each form of expression whose row reads as no `io`, evaluated with fuel `n + 1`, keeps the world when its parts, which the evaluator runs with fuel `n`, do. |  |
| [`Stilt.Quiet.Forms`](Stilt/Quiet/Forms.lean) | A `match` runs its arms, a loop its body's turns, a `when` its block between taking its cells and putting their values back, and a `with` its block and its handler's clauses, whose rows fit within the row around the `with` (handlers.handler-literals): each keeps the world when what it runs does. |  |
| [`Stilt.Quiet.Handlers`](Stilt/Quiet/Handlers.lean) | The clause of a handler for an operation is a closure whose row, but for its `resume`, fits within the row around the `with` (handlers.handler-literals). |  |
| [`Stilt.Quiet.Handling`](Stilt/Quiet/Handling.lean) | An operation other than a read or a write of the world leaves its output, standard error and input as they were: `Shared.new` makes a cell, and the others leave the world alone. |  |
| [`Stilt.Quiet.Outcome`](Stilt/Quiet/Outcome.lean) | `Outcome.KeepsIo D X c o`: every state that `o` carries has the output, the standard error and the input `c`, and the rest of each performance, resumed in a typed world with a value of each type its operation may give, keeps that world's. | [std.io](../reference/17-std.md#std.io) |
| [`Stilt.Quiet.Steps`](Stilt/Quiet/Steps.lean) | `Quiet D n`: every instantiation of a well-typed expression whose row reads as no `io` at run time, evaluated with fuel `n` from a typed state, keeps the world's output, standard error and input (`Eval.KeepsIo`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Quiet.Theorem`](Stilt/Quiet/Theorem.lean) | **Quiet code** (facts.io-and-capabilities): every instantiation of a well-typed expression whose row reads as no `io` at run time, evaluated with any fuel from a typed state, ends with the world's output, standard error and input as they were, and so does the rest of each performance it makes, from the world it is resumed in (`Decls.Typed.quiet`). | [facts.io-and-capabilities](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.io-and-capabilities) |
| [`Stilt.Quiet.With`](Stilt/Quiet/With.lean) | A `with` whose block's row reads as no `io` has clauses whose rows read as none, since each fits within the row around the `with` (handlers.handler-literals). |  |
| [`Stilt.Receivers`](Stilt/Receivers.lean) | A method call `recv.m(args)` is the call `T.m(recv, args)` of the method as a function, whose first parameter is the receiver (traits.method-resolution and traits.qualified-calls). | [modes.overlapping-inout](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.overlapping-inout), [modes.durations](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.durations), [expressions.inout-arguments](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.inout-arguments), [modes.kinds-of-access](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.kinds-of-access), [modes.what-can-be-modified](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.what-can-be-modified), [values.passing-values](../reference/05-values.md#values.passing-values) |
| [`Stilt.Related`](Stilt/Related.lean) | A simulation says that two runs, from states that correspond, end alike: two models of memory running the same program, or one model running a program and its erasure. |  |
| [`Stilt.Rename`](Stilt/Rename.lean) | `Expr.rename g` writes every variable of an expression as `g` gives it: those it reads, the roots of the places it writes or passes `inout`, and those it binds. |  |
| [`Stilt.Rows`](Stilt/Rows.lean) | The instantiation of row variables in an expression, at a use (effects.row-variables and effects.inference), and the lemmas on rows and on fitting types (effects.missing-effects, effects.function-value-unions and effects.function-values). | [effects.row-variables](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.row-variables), [effects.function-values](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.function-values), [effects.catch](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.catch), [effects.function-value-unions](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.function-value-unions), [expressions.statements](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.statements) |
| [`Stilt.Rows.Types`](Stilt/Rows/Types.lean) | Effect rows and the relations on them that effects.rows and effects.function-values defines: one row fitting within another (effects.missing-effects), the union of the rows of branches (effects.function-value-unions), a function value fitting a function type with a larger row (effects.function-values), the rows a type holds, and the instantiation of row variables, written and implicit, in a type at a use (effects.row-variables and effects.inference). | [effects.rows](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.rows), [effects.missing-effects](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.missing-effects), [effects.one-error-type](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.one-error-type), [effects.catch](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.catch), [effects.row-variables](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.row-variables), [effects.function-value-unions](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.function-value-unions), [declarations.never](../reference/10-declarations.md#declarations.never), [effects.function-values](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.function-values), [effects.non-escaping-parameters](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.non-escaping-parameters), [effects.paired-row-variables](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.paired-row-variables), [effects.catch-around-row-variables](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.catch-around-row-variables), [effects.passing-to-paired-variables](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.passing-to-paired-variables), [traits.unsatisfied-bounds](../reference/11-traits.md#traits.unsatisfied-bounds), [views.viewable-types](../reference/13-modes.md#views.viewable-types), [declarations.function-declarations](../reference/10-declarations.md#declarations.function-declarations), [effects.inference](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.inference), [handlers.rows-pass-effects-on](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.rows-pass-effects-on), [handlers.resuming](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.resuming) |

| [`Stilt.Run.Program`](Stilt/Run/Program.lean) | A program read from JSON (`Stilt.Run.Decode`), run on its input in the models of memory of copies, of in-place mutation, of windows when it makes a view and of sharing when it may share, and what the run shows as a process would: its standard output, its exit status and what it writes to standard error (program.exit-statuses). | [facts.without](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.without), [program.exit-statuses](../reference/04-programs.md#program.exit-statuses), [views.making-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.making-view) |
| [`Stilt.Scope`](Stilt/Scope.lean) | Running code with variables bound, and ending the bindings however the code ends (expressions.scope): a `let`'s or a `var`'s, which may take over the storage of a variable at its last use (values.passing-values), and the names a pattern binds. | [expressions.scope](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.scope), [values.let-and-var](../reference/05-values.md#values.let-and-var), [values.passing-values](../reference/05-values.md#values.passing-values) |

| [`Stilt.Shared.Deadlock`](Stilt/Shared/Deadlock.lean) | shared.when-order and shared.locks: a `when` waits until it holds every value it names, all at once, and a `when` whose block runs another is E0430, so a task never waits while it holds a value. | [shared.when](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when), [shared.locks](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.locks), [shared.when-order](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when-order) |
| [`Stilt.Shared.Fold`](Stilt/Shared/Fold.lean) | par.fold-tree: `par.fold` combines a list of `n` elements in a balanced tree that depends on `n` alone, its first `⌊n/2⌋` elements folded, combined with the rest folded. | [par.fold-tree](../reference/14-tasks.md#par.fold-tree) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Agreement`](Stilt/Sharing/Agreement.lean) | The simulation of copies by sharing, form by form: every evaluation of a safe expression ends alike in both models when every binding is shared (modes.shared-writes), each binding reading in the sharing model's store what it holds as a copy. | [shared.when](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when), [modes.shared-writes](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.shared-writes) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Alias`](Stilt/Sharing/Alias.lean) | A `let` or a `var` whose initializer is a place, at a copy-on-write type, refers to the place (modes.copies): evaluating such a place reads it and changes nothing (`eval_alias`), so the place holds the initializer's value when the binding is made, and the binding agrees with the copy that copy-in, copy-out binds. |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Assign`](Stilt/Sharing/Assign.lean) | An assignment of the whole of a binding either redirects a local of a copy-on-write type to the place it is given (modes.copies), or gives every binding that referred into its home a copy of its own and writes the value at the home, copying nothing (modes.shared-writes). | [modes.copies](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.copies), [modes.shared-writes](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.shared-writes) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Assignment`](Stilt/Sharing/Assignment.lean) | An assignment runs alike with copies and with sharing when every binding is shared: its indexes and its value run alike, and the write then changes the binding's value alone, either the whole of it or a part (values.assignment-order, modes.shared-writes). | [values.assignment-order](../reference/05-values.md#values.assignment-order), [modes.shared-writes](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.shared-writes) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Bind`](Stilt/Sharing/Bind.lean) | What a new binding and the end of one do to a frame of copies and a state of sharing that agree: a binding with a new home holds its value there, and one of a place refers to the place, which holds the same value (modes.copies); ending the newest binding ends it in both (expressions.scope). |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Call`](Stilt/Sharing/Call.lean) | A call keeps the simulation when every binding is shared: each root of an `inout` argument stops sharing when the call starts (`unshareArgs`), the callee starts alike (`Stilt.Sharing.Enter`), its body runs alike, and what copy-out writes back when it ends is what sharing wrote in place (`Stilt.Sharing.Leave`). | [modes.shared-writes](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.shared-writes) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Capture`](Stilt/Sharing/Capture.lean) | A closure literal captures the same values with copies and with sharing: copy-in, copy-out keeps the newest binding of each variable of its frame that the closure captures, and sharing reads those bindings where they refer, which a frame that agrees on every binding holds alike (expressions.capture). | [expressions.capture](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.capture) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Enter`](Stilt/Sharing/Enter.lean) | What sharing does when a call starts (values.inout-argument-order): the callee's captured variables and value parameters get new homes, and each `inout` parameter's home is its argument's place, below its root's home, which no other binding of the caller overlaps once `unshareArgs` has run. |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Evacuate`](Stilt/Sharing/Evacuate.lean) | When every binding of a frame reads a value, `evacuate` succeeds, each binding reads what it read, and the bindings it settles get new locations of their own, so the frame's homes still don't overlap (modes.shared-writes). |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Eval`](Stilt/Sharing/Eval.lean) | The evaluator of `Stilt.Semantics` in the model `Sharing.memory`, but where a `let` or a `var` binds, where an assignment writes and where a call starts. | [modes.kinds-of-access](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.kinds-of-access), [modes.shared-writes](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.shared-writes), [modes.copies](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.copies), [expressions.scope](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.scope) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Evolves`](Stilt/Sharing/Evolves.lean) | `Evolves s s'`: the store of `s'` comes from the store of `s` by a sequence of writes, each below the home of one of the bindings of `s` or below the part one refers to, and by locations past the end of the store, which may hold anything. |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Frame`](Stilt/Sharing/Frame.lean) | Facts on the bindings of a state of sharing that the steps changing them need: a frame whose bindings, position by position, read what another's read agrees with the same frame of copies; homes that don't overlap are distinct; and `evacuate` gives each binding it settles a new location of its own, so the homes still don't overlap. |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Handling`](Stilt/Sharing/Handling.lean) | A performance ends alike with copies and with sharing, and a `with` handles alike what its block ends with: its clauses are calls of the same safe values on the same safe values, and what a clause or the rest of the block ends with relates the frame and the state it ends with, wherever the other runs left off (handlers.performing-operation and handlers.with). | [handlers.performing-operation](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.performing-operation), [handlers.with](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.with) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Holding`](Stilt/Sharing/Holding.lean) | Copies and sharing run a `when` alike: they read the same values of its names and put them back in one world, and a `with` lets the same cells go. |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Kept`](Stilt/Sharing/Kept.lean) | The binding of an `inout` parameter has its argument's place for home all through the call: it never refers elsewhere (`assignWhole` redirects only a binding that isn't `inout`), `evacuate` leaves it alone (its home is apart from every other), and an assignment keeps its home. |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Leave`](Stilt/Sharing/Leave.lean) | When a call ends, copy-in, copy-out writes each `inout` parameter's final value back to its argument's place, from left to right (values.inout-arguments). |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Let`](Stilt/Sharing/Let.lean) | A `let` or a `var` runs alike with copies and with sharing when every binding is shared: its initializer runs alike, and its body runs with the name bound, in a new home or, for an initializer that names a place at a copy-on-write type, referring to the place, which holds the initializer's value (modes.copies). | [modes.copies](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.copies) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Memory`](Stilt/Sharing/Memory.lean) | A further model of memory, `Sharing`, in which a copy of a binding, or of a part of one, into another binding shares the storage it copies, and a write copies first only where the inference of modes.shared-writes says the binding is shared. | [modes.shared-writes](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.shared-writes), [modes.copy-on-write-types](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.copy-on-write-types), [values.independent-copies](../reference/05-values.md#values.independent-copies), [expressions.capture](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.capture), [modes.copies](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.copies), [facts.copy](../reference/15-facts.md#facts.copy), [modes.kinds-of-access](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.kinds-of-access) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Modify`](Stilt/Sharing/Modify.lean) | A modification of a binding that the inference calls `shared` first `unshare`s it, after which no other binding's part overlaps its home, so the write changes its value alone, as the copy model's write does (modes.shared-writes). | [modes.shared-writes](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.shared-writes) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Names`](Stilt/Sharing/Names.lean) | The names of a program's bindings whose modifications the inference of modes.shared-writes calls shared: in each body, a function's, `main`'s or a closure literal's, the bindings that `ModeEnv.bodyShared` gives, as the facts of `Stilt.Modes.Facts` read each body once (modes.bindings-and-axes). | [modes.shared-writes](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.shared-writes) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Relation`](Stilt/Sharing/Relation.lean) | `Agrees m fr s`: a frame of copy-in, copy-out and a state of sharing agree on the bindings that the mask `m` keeps live, as `Stilt.Moves.Relation` reads a mask: each live binding holds in the copy frame the value its binding of sharing reads where it refers. |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Roots`](Stilt/Sharing/Roots.lean) | When a call starts, each root of an `inout` argument that is shared stops sharing (modes.kinds-of-access): it refers to its home, which no other binding's part overlaps, so the callee's parameters, which refer below that home, are the only way to its storage. | [modes.kinds-of-access](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.kinds-of-access) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Scope`](Stilt/Sharing/Scope.lean) | A body run with one more binding, which ends however the body ends (expressions.scope), runs alike with copies and with sharing: a `catch`'s, a `for`'s and an arm's binders, each with a new home, and a `let`'s or a `var`'s, which shares the place its initializer names (modes.copies). |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Steps`](Stilt/Sharing/Steps.lean) | The simulation of copy-in, copy-out by sharing when every binding is shared, and the steps of the evaluator that keep it, as `Stilt.InPlace.Steps` gives them for in-place mutation. | [expressions.break-and-continue](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.break-and-continue) |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Store`](Stilt/Sharing/Store.lean) | What reading and writing the store of `Stilt.Sharing.Memory` do: a write leaves every part that doesn't overlap the part written as it was, a new location leaves the others as they were, and a binding given a copy of its own (`settle`), or every binding that refers into a part (`evacuate`), reads what it read before, from a new location. |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Unshare`](Stilt/Sharing/Unshare.lean) | What `unshare`, `modify` and `assignWhole` of `Stilt.Sharing.Memory` do to a frame: a binding that stops sharing gets a copy of its own when it referred to another's storage, and otherwise every other binding that referred into its home does; a write the inference calls `unique` writes where its binding refers, and a shared one stops sharing first (modes.shared-writes). |  |
| [`Stilt.Sharing.Unshared`](Stilt/Sharing/Unshared.lean) | When a frame of copies and a state of sharing agree on every binding, `unshare` succeeds, every binding reads what it read, and the binding that stops sharing refers to its home, which no other binding's part overlaps: a binding that referred to another's storage gets a new home, and otherwise every other binding that referred into its home gets one (modes.shared-writes). | [modes.shared-writes](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.shared-writes) |
| [`Stilt.Show`](Stilt/Show.lean) | The output of the prelude's structural impls of `Show` (std.show-output and traits.show): a value's text, and its text nested in another value, where a string is quoted and escaped (std.show-names-and-escapes). | [std.show-names-and-escapes](../reference/17-std.md#std.show-names-and-escapes), [records.field-order](../reference/07-types.md#records.field-order) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Arguments`](Stilt/Soundness/Arguments.lean) | A generic record or variant type's fields and payloads are declared in terms of its type parameters (declarations.type-parameters), and a value of an instance has them at the instance's arguments. |  |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Call`](Stilt/Soundness/Call.lean) | A call of a function value runs its body as the value's type promises: a closure's body in the frame it captured, under the environment of the body that made it, and a declared function's body under the environment that its use composed. | [declarations.function-declarations](../reference/10-declarations.md#declarations.function-declarations), [effects.inference](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.inference), [expressions.closure-boundaries](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.closure-boundaries), [effects.missing-effects](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.missing-effects), [effects.catch-around-row-variables](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.catch-around-row-variables) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Cases`](Stilt/Soundness/Cases.lean) | Each form of expression, evaluated with fuel `n + 1`, ends soundly when its parts, which the evaluator runs with fuel `n`, do. |  |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Cells`](Stilt/Soundness/Cells.lean) | What keeps a world typed while `when`s hold and put back its cells: holding a cell, putting back a value of its type, and letting cells go change no cell's type and leave each cell a value of its type (shared.when). |  |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Close`](Stilt/Soundness/Close.lean) | How `Ty.close` reads the relations of `Stilt.Rows`: a row that fits within another raises no more error types, `Fits` gives a run-time type that fits, a closed type reads the same under every environment, and instantiating a type's row variables, then reading it, is reading it under the composed environment, up to equivalence. | [effects.inference](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.inference) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Compose`](Stilt/Soundness/Compose.lean) | A use instantiates the row variables of a function with `s` (effects.inference), and the body runs under the environment that `s` composes with the use's own (`RowSubst.compose`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Forms`](Stilt/Soundness/Forms.lean) | The forms of expressions that build or take apart values of declared types. |  |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Frames`](Stilt/Soundness/Frames.lean) | A call starts once its arguments are evaluated: it pairs each parameter with its argument, and builds the callee's frame from the captured variables, a copy of each `let` argument and the value of each `inout` argument's place (values.inout-argument-order). | [effects.inout-function-arguments](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.inout-function-arguments), [values.inout-arguments](../reference/05-values.md#values.inout-arguments), [values.inout-argument-order](../reference/05-values.md#values.inout-argument-order) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Handlers`](Stilt/Soundness/Handlers.lean) | A performance of an operation whose effect the row holds ends as a performance that the exits allow, on an argument of the operation's type, and its rest, resumed with a value of the operation's result type, ends with that value (handlers.performing-operation and handlers.resuming). | [handlers.handler-literals](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.handler-literals), [handlers.resuming](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.resuming) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Holding`](Stilt/Soundness/Holding.lean) | A `when` evaluates its handles, values of `Shared<T>`, so each names a cell whose values are those of its `T`; it holds them, or panics with `deadlock`; it binds each name to its cell's value, a value of its `T`; and however its block ends it puts each name's value back in its cell, a value of the cell's type, so the world stays typed (shared.when). |  |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Identity`](Stilt/Soundness/Identity.lean) | Instantiating no row variable changes nothing. |  |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Inhabited`](Stilt/Soundness/Inhabited.lean) | Every value of a type has a type with values (patterns.types-without-values): a record's fields, a variant's payload and a tuple's elements are values of their types, which are therefore inhabited, and so is the type that holds them. |  |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Lists`](Stilt/Soundness/Lists.lean) | The evaluator's combinators that run several expressions in turn: the elements of a list or a payload, the fields of a record literal, the arguments of a call and the indexes of a place (values.left-to-right). | [values.left-to-right](../reference/05-values.md#values.left-to-right), [expressions.struct-literal-fields](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.struct-literal-fields) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Loops`](Stilt/Soundness/Loops.lean) | A `while` and a `loop` run turns of their body, each followed by the loop again, which rests on the lemma at the fuel below for the loop itself. | [expressions.loop-types](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.loop-types) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Maps`](Stilt/Soundness/Maps.lean) | What a built-in operation ends with, and the soundness of the functions of maps and sets. | [numbers.lengths-are-i64](../reference/06-numbers.md#numbers.lengths-are-i64) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Methods`](Stilt/Soundness/Methods.lean) | `Trait.m` at a type with an impl of the trait, an impl of `Eq`, `Ord` or `Show` that the program writes among them, is the function of that impl, whose signature is the method's with `Self` the type (traits.impl-signatures). |  |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Ops`](Stilt/Soundness/Ops.lean) | An operation applied to values of the types its signature takes gives a value of the type it gives, or panics; it is never stuck. | [views.making-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.making-view) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Outcome`](Stilt/Soundness/Outcome.lean) | What type soundness allows a step of the copy-in, copy-out evaluator to end with, from a frame of its context: a value that the step's property holds of, a raise of an error type that its effects allow, or a jump that they allow, each with a frame of the same context; a panic; or running out of fuel. | [effects.catch-around-row-variables](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.catch-around-row-variables), [effects.missing-effects](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.missing-effects), [handlers.performing-operation](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.performing-operation), [handlers.resuming](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.resuming), [shared.std-sync](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.std-sync), [expressions.break-and-continue](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.break-and-continue), [expressions.closure-boundaries](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.closure-boundaries), [effects.panic-kinds](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.panic-kinds), [expressions.scope](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.scope), [values.inout-arguments](../reference/05-values.md#values.inout-arguments) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Paths`](Stilt/Soundness/Paths.lean) | A place's path, once its indexes are evaluated, leads through values of the types that typing gave its steps (`PathTyped`). | [types.field-access](../reference/07-types.md#types.field-access), [std.lists](../reference/17-std.md#std.lists) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Patterns`](Stilt/Soundness/Patterns.lean) | A pattern that fits a type and matches a value of it binds values of the types its typing gives its names (patterns.pattern-types and patterns.bindings-are-copies), and arms that the usefulness algorithm finds exhaustive match every value of the type (patterns.algorithm): a well-typed `match` always finds an arm. | [patterns.pattern-types](../reference/09-patterns.md#patterns.pattern-types), [patterns.pattern-forms](../reference/09-patterns.md#patterns.pattern-forms), [patterns.algorithm](../reference/09-patterns.md#patterns.algorithm), [patterns.guards-and-constants](../reference/09-patterns.md#patterns.guards-and-constants) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Theorem`](Stilt/Soundness/Theorem.lean) | The fundamental lemma: every instantiation of a well-typed expression, evaluated with any fuel from a frame of its context, under an environment that keeps its scope's promises, ends as `Outcome.Ok` says. | [program.main](../reference/04-programs.md#program.main), [expressions.break-and-continue](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.break-and-continue), [expressions.closure-boundaries](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.closure-boundaries) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Types`](Stilt/Soundness/Types.lean) | The types that values have while a program runs. | [effects.one-error-type](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.one-error-type), [effects.inference](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.inference), [handlers.effects-in-rows](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.effects-in-rows), [effects.rows](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.rows), [types.no-type-arguments](../reference/07-types.md#types.no-type-arguments), [handlers.resuming](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.resuming), [shared.std-sync](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.std-sync), [effects.function-values](../reference/12-effects.md#effects.function-values), [declarations.never](../reference/10-declarations.md#declarations.never) |
| [`Stilt.Soundness.Uses`](Stilt/Soundness/Uses.lean) | A use of a declared function instantiates its row variables (effects.inference), and the function's body runs under the environment that the instantiation composes with the use's own. |  |

| [`Stilt.Sources`](Stilt/Sources.lean) | The places a view reads, its **sources** (views.making-view), as the checks of `Stilt.ViewReads` and `Stilt.ViewContained` follow them through a body. | [views.view-types](../reference/13-modes.md#views.view-types), [views.escaping](../reference/13-modes.md#views.escaping), [views.fixed](../reference/13-modes.md#views.fixed), [views.viewable-types](../reference/13-modes.md#views.viewable-types), [views.across-calls](../reference/13-modes.md#views.across-calls), [shared.when](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when), [views.making-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.making-view) |

| [`Stilt.Strengthen`](Stilt/Strengthen.lean) | The typing judgment reads a context only at the variables an expression uses (`Expr.free`): a context that gives each of them the same binding types the expression alike. | [expressions.capture](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.capture) |
| [`Stilt.Strings`](Stilt/Strings.lean) | What the natives of `str` that the calculus holds compute: its length in bytes, slicing between byte offsets, the tests for a substring, and the order of strings by their bytes (std.bytes-and-scalars, std.str, std.str-search and traits.built-in-impls). | [std.str](../reference/17-std.md#std.str), [numbers.lengths-are-i64](../reference/06-numbers.md#numbers.lengths-are-i64), [std.str-search](../reference/17-std.md#std.str-search), [traits.built-in-impls](../reference/11-traits.md#traits.built-in-impls) |

| [`Stilt.Views.Syntax`](Stilt/Views/Syntax.lean) | views.what-view-is adds views, windows into part of a value that someone else owns (views.what-view-is). | [views.std-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.std-view), [views.view-types](../reference/13-modes.md#views.view-types), [views.viewable-types](../reference/13-modes.md#views.viewable-types), [expressions.statements](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.statements), [expressions.assignment](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.assignment), [modes.places](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.places), [views.functions-of-views](../reference/13-modes.md#views.functions-of-views), [types.field-access](../reference/07-types.md#types.field-access), [expressions.for-iterables](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.for-iterables), [views.for-over-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.for-over-view), [expressions.if](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.if), [std.lists](../reference/17-std.md#std.lists), [values.let-and-var](../reference/05-values.md#values.let-and-var), [expressions.struct-literal-fields](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.struct-literal-fields), [views.making-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.making-view), [modes.overlap](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.overlap), [views.across-calls](../reference/13-modes.md#views.across-calls) |
| [`Stilt.Views.Typing`](Stilt/Views/Typing.lean) | The typing judgment of the calculus of views. | [modes.what-can-be-modified](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.what-can-be-modified), [views.functions-of-views](../reference/13-modes.md#views.functions-of-views), [views.for-over-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.for-over-view), [expressions.struct-literal-fields](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.struct-literal-fields), [views.viewable-types](../reference/13-modes.md#views.viewable-types), [views.making-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.making-view), [expressions.mutable-places](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.mutable-places), [projections.places-through-views](../reference/13-modes.md#projections.places-through-views) |
| [`Stilt.Views.Within`](Stilt/Views/Within.lean) | Running the rest of a block with a binding, in both models, ends with states that agree and a step under the reads around the block. |  |
| [`Stilt.Weakening`](Stilt/Weakening.lean) | The typing judgment is preserved by bindings added below a context, which its own bindings hide or which nothing names, and by a larger row scope, one whose relations hold of more variables. |  |
| [`Stilt.WellTyped`](Stilt/WellTyped.lean) | Well-formed declarations, with the implicit row variables of their non-escaping parameters, and functions and programs whose bodies the checker accepts in the scope that a version of the rules gives them: the rules as effects.paired-row-variables and effects.passing-to-paired-variables amend them, effects.paired-row-variables without effects.passing-to-paired-variables, and effects.catch-around-row-variables as it was written, each with the no-escape rule. | | [`Stilt.Whens`](Stilt/Whens.lean) | Every `when` that code writes, at any depth, those in closure literals and handlers' bodies included: its names, each with the type of the value its handle reaches, and its block (shared.when). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Agree`](Stilt/Windows/Agree.lean) | Binding a variable keeps two states that agree; so does ending a binding whose location no window reads, freeing locations that no window on a location before them reads, and changing both worlds alike. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Assign`](Stilt/Windows/Assign.lean) | An assignment `x.ps = e` (values.assignment-order): the indexes of the place run, then the value, then both models write it at the same path below `x` (values.let-and-var). | [values.assignment-order](../reference/05-values.md#values.assignment-order) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.AssignFits`](Stilt/Windows/AssignFits.lean) | A variable that an assignment of a view writes is one of the body's own with a written type, so it is one of the newest variables, and every variable that a source of the view names, which the checks know to outlive it, is older (`Fits.named`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.AssignStore`](Stilt/Windows/AssignStore.lean) | A write at a path of a value, through windows or not, leaves what is read along a disjoint path (`getW_setW_disjoint`), and what is read along a prefix of the path is the part there with the rest written (`getW_setW_prefix`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.AssignVars`](Stilt/Windows/AssignVars.lean) | What the simulation knows of each variable holds after a write that the checks of views accept. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.AssignWrite`](Stilt/Windows/AssignWrite.lean) | An assignment `x.ps = e`, run in both models, writes the value of `e` into `x`'s location at the same path. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Bind`](Stilt/Windows/Bind.lean) | A variable of the body bound at a new location, the one past the store's end, with what the checks know of it: the states keep the simulation in the scope of the binding, whose reads hide the variable's name and hold its sources (views.reads-that-last). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.Args`](Stilt/Windows/Call/Args.lean) | A call's arguments run from left to right (values.left-to-right): a value argument's value waits while those after it run, its sources being read (`argsViewsRead`), and an `inout` argument's indexes give the same path in both models (values.index-reads). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.Call`](Stilt/Windows/Call/Call.lean) | A call evaluates its callee, then its arguments from left to right, the callee's value waiting while they run (values.left-to-right), then calls the callee's value: in the model of windows with its `let` arguments kept for its parameters' types, its locations freed when it ends, and its result read through unless the call's type is a view type (views.across-calls). | [views.across-calls](../reference/13-modes.md#views.across-calls) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.Callee`](Stilt/Windows/Call/Callee.lean) | A call of a closure or of a declared function runs its body as `Eval.invoke` does, in the context the checks read it in: a function's parameters (`FnDecl.viewScope`), or a closure's parameters on top of the variables around its literal (`ViewScope.enter`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.Enter`](Stilt/Windows/Call/Enter.lean) | Both models start a call alike (`enter_rel`): they fail alike, or give the callee's states, from which its simulation runs in the callee's context (`Ctx.callee`): the scope of its parameters over the captured variables, no read around it, the values its `let` parameters were bound to as what they brought in, the caller's store length as the start of its own locations, the result's parameters, and each caller location's cell at the start as what it held then. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.EnterArgs`](Stilt/Windows/Call/EnterArgs.lean) | Once a call's arguments are passed, the model of windows having kept each `let` argument for its parameter's type, both models bind the same parameters to arguments of the same shapes (`bindArgs`): values read alike, the in-place one a value the checks accept, each window of the other watched by the reads around the call, and kept only for a parameter whose generic type holds a view and is no function type, so whose binding's sources are its own place; and the same places, whose shapes the reads around the call don't overlap (E0423). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.EnterFold`](Stilt/Windows/Call/EnterFold.lean) | Both models bind a call's parameters alike: a `let` parameter at a new location, an `inout` one to its argument's place, which both read alike, failing alike (`place_step`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.EnterStep`](Stilt/Windows/Call/EnterStep.lean) | Both models start a call alike (`enter`): the captured values and the `let` arguments at new locations, each `inout` parameter a reference to its argument's place, whose location the model of windows first reads through its windows. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.Leave`](Stilt/Windows/Call/Leave.lean) | When a call ends, the caller goes on with its own variables, the store the callee left with the callee's locations freed, and the world the callee left (values.inout-arguments, views.across-calls). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.Pass`](Stilt/Windows/Call/Pass.lean) | Once its arguments are evaluated, a call of the model of windows keeps each `let` argument for its parameter's type and reads the others through (`passTo`), which changes no state. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.Reads`](Stilt/Windows/Call/Reads.lean) | What a read through windows gives when every window it may meet reads a part that holds no window: a value that holds none, or what the same read gives in any store, since it met no window on the way. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.Settle`](Stilt/Windows/Call/Settle.lean) | A callee's outcome becomes its call's (`Outcome.leaveCall`): the caller goes on with its own variables and the store the callee left, and in the model of windows the callee's locations are freed (`Outcome.settle`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Call.Value`](Stilt/Windows/Call/Value.lean) | A call binds its parameters to the arguments passed (`bindArgs`), which have the same shapes in both models, starts the callee (`enter`), runs its body, and ends as `Outcome.leaveCall` and, in the model of windows, `Outcome.settle` say (values.inout-arguments, views.across-calls). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Checked`](Stilt/Windows/Checked.lean) | What a call needs of the function it calls: its parameters named once each, its body's closure literals too, its body obeying the law and accepted by both checks of views in the scope of its parameters. | [views.across-calls](../reference/13-modes.md#views.across-calls) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Closure`](Stilt/Windows/Closure.lean) | A closure captures what its variables hold, and the model of windows what their windows read (expressions.capture): both models capture the same values, which the checks accept, or fail alike, so both make the same closure, one the checks of its literal accept (views.fixed). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Control`](Stilt/Windows/Control.lean) | The forms that pass control on, on the relation of windows: a turn of a loop's body, which a `break` or a `continue` ends (expressions.break-and-continue); a raise (effects.raise) and a `return` of a value whose windows the result's parameters track (views.escaping); a `catch`, whose error is read through its windows and bound in the handler's scope (effects.catch, effects.innermost-catch); and a `for` loop, whose iterable is read through its windows and each element bound in the body's scope (expressions.for-iterables, views.for-over-view). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Effects`](Stilt/Windows/Effects.lean) | The forms that leave the body or come back to it, on the relation of windows: a raised or returned value's sources are the result's parameters (`leaves`), a caught error and a performed value are read through their windows, and a handler's clauses are called as a call of a value is (`CallsValues`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Eval`](Stilt/Windows/Eval.lean) | The evaluator of `Stilt.Semantics` with views made as windows (views.making-view): it is `eval` in the model `Windows.memory` but where a view is made, where a window is read, and where a value leaves the frames it was made in. | [views.viewable-types](../reference/13-modes.md#views.viewable-types), [views.making-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.making-view), [views.view-types](../reference/13-modes.md#views.view-types), [views.across-calls](../reference/13-modes.md#views.across-calls), [views.escaping](../reference/13-modes.md#views.escaping), [handlers.resuming](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.resuming), [expressions.for-iterables](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.for-iterables), [views.for-over-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.for-over-view) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Fields`](Stilt/Windows/Fields.lean) | The fields of a record literal run in turn as the operands of a list do; a record is built from fields read alike; and the indexes of a place give the same path in both models, an index read through a window being no integer in either. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Fits`](Stilt/Windows/Fits.lean) | Lemmas on `Tracked`, `Unreached` and `Fits`, and how they give a window guarded by the reads around: one that no variable reaches is guarded by any reads, and one that a value's sources track, when the reads hold those sources and distinct variables reach disjoint parts of the store (`guarded_of_tracked`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Forms`](Stilt/Windows/Forms.lean) | Each form a step of both evaluators, on the relation of windows: from runs of its parts at the fuel below, which keep the simulation in every context the checks read (`RunsAll`), both models end alike, with values that the form's sources track. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Guards.Call`](Stilt/Windows/Guards/Call.lean) | In place, a callee's variables are new locations, except that each `inout` parameter refers to its argument's place in the caller's store (`Entry`). | [handlers.with](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.with) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Guards.Checks`](Stilt/Windows/Guards/Checks.lean) | `Expr.viewsRead` checks the operands of a form in turn, each under the places read around it and the sources of the views among the operands before it. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Guards.Eval`](Stilt/Windows/Guards/Eval.lean) | `eval_guards`: when E0423 accepts `e` with the places `R` read around it (views.change-while-viewed, `Expr.viewsRead`), evaluating `e` in place, with any fuel, from a valid state whose regions `G` the reads `R` guard, writes only at paths disjoint from each region of `G` and at new locations, in every outcome and in every rest of a performance. | [views.change-while-viewed](../reference/13-modes.md#views.change-while-viewed) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Guards.Evolves`](Stilt/Windows/Guards/Evolves.lean) | `EvolvesBy ok s s'` is `Evolves s s'` with each write at a path that `ok` allows: the variables are the same, and the store comes from `s`'s by writes below the references of `s`'s variables, each at an allowed path, and by new locations past its end. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Guards.Outcome`](Stilt/Windows/Guards/Outcome.lean) | `o.Within ok P s`: every state the in-place outcome `o` ends in evolved from `s` by writes that `ok` allows (`EvolvesBy`), a value it ends with satisfies `P`, and each rest of a performance, resumed with any value in any world, ends so again. | [handlers.with](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.with), [shared.when](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Guards.Parts`](Stilt/Windows/Guards/Parts.lean) | What the guard of the views clause gives a window: when its reference is a region that the evaluation avoids, the in-place store reads it the same part after the evaluation as before (`partIn_evolvesBy`), and a value whose windows are all such regions reads the same against both stores (`resolveIn_evolvesBy`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Guards.Regions`](Stilt/Windows/Guards/Regions.lean) | A part of the in-place store, named by its path from the store read as a list, is `Guarded` by the places `R` being read when every variable in scope reaches it, or a part that holds it, or a part within it, only along a path whose shape overlaps one of `R` (modes.overlap). | [modes.overlap](../reference/13-modes.md#modes.overlap) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Guards.Runs`](Stilt/Windows/Guards/Runs.lean) | The steps of `Stilt.Semantics` that run expressions through `run`: lists, fields, the indexes of a place, a call's arguments, the arms of a `match`, an assignment and a `for` loop. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Guards.Steps`](Stilt/Windows/Guards/Steps.lean) | `Eval.Guards G R P m`: from every valid in-place state whose store has the regions `G` guarded by the reads `R` (`Regions`), every outcome of `m` evolves by writes disjoint from each region of `G`, the rests of its performances included, and its values satisfy `P`. | [shared.when](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Guards.Writes`](Stilt/Windows/Guards/Writes.lean) | `eval_writes`: evaluating any expression in place, with any fuel, from a valid state, ends in states that evolved by writes below the references of its variables and by new locations, the rests of its performances included (`Eval.Guards` with no region). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Handling`](Stilt/Windows/Handling.lean) | A performance of the same value, which holds no window, is resumed alike in both models (handlers.performing-operation). | [handlers.with](../reference/12-effects.md#handlers.with) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Handling.Escapes`](Stilt/Windows/Handling/Escapes.lean) | A value the checks accept holds no window, nor does a value read through its windows. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Held`](Stilt/Windows/Held.lean) | A `when` on handles read alike holds the same cells in both models: a handle is no window and reads as itself, and a window's part is a string or a list, which names no cell. | [shared.when](../reference/14-tasks.md#shared.when) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Inst`](Stilt/Windows/Inst.lean) | A function's body runs with its type parameters and row variables instantiated, while the checks of views read the body as it is declared. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Kept`](Stilt/Windows/Kept.lean) | A change of the store of windows that keeps a location's cell keeps what a window on it reads, and what is read along a path through windows whose regions it keeps (`getW_congr`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Let`](Stilt/Windows/Let.lean) | A binding's initializer runs, its value is kept for the binding's type (`keep`: read through its windows unless the type keeps views), and the body runs in the binding's scope, whose reads hold the binding's sources (views.reads-that-last). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Lists`](Stilt/Windows/Lists.lean) | The operands of a list, a variant, an operation, a view or a call, and the fields of a record literal, run from left to right; each one's value waits while those after it run, with its sources being read (`exprsViewsRead`, `fieldsViewsRead`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Match`](Stilt/Windows/Match.lean) | The model of windows reads the scrutinee through its windows (views.functions-of-views), so both models match the same value, which holds no window, against the same patterns, and bind the same parts in the arm they pick (patterns.match), each binder in a scope of its own that reads what the scrutinee reads and hides its name. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Memory`](Stilt/Windows/Memory.lean) | A fourth model of memory, `Windows`, in which a view is a window into its source's storage and holds no copy of the part it reads (views.what-view-is). | [views.what-view-is](../reference/13-modes.md#views.what-view-is), [views.making-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.making-view), [views.view-types](../reference/13-modes.md#views.view-types), [values.independent-copies](../reference/05-values.md#values.independent-copies), [values.let-and-var](../reference/05-values.md#values.let-and-var), [values.assignment-order](../reference/05-values.md#values.assignment-order), [expressions.scope](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.scope), [expressions.capture](../reference/08-expressions.md#expressions.capture) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Parts`](Stilt/Windows/Parts.lean) | The model of windows reads a value through its windows before taking a field or an element of it (views.functions-of-views), so both models take the same part of the same value, which holds no window. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Path`](Stilt/Windows/Path.lean) | A place's indexes run in turn, each read as an integer alike, so both models give the same path, whose fields are the place's (`Path.Resolves`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Read`](Stilt/Windows/Read.lean) | A variable names the same location in both models, which holds related values: read along the same path, they give related values, or fail alike (`getW_rel`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Relation`](Stilt/Windows/Relation.lean) | What the agreement of the model of windows with the in-place model keeps of their states and values while both run a body that the checks of views accept. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Resolve`](Stilt/Windows/Resolve.lean) | What the agreement of windows with in-place mutation reads a value of the model of windows as: each of its windows replaced by the part that the in-place store holds there (`resolveIn`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Scalars`](Stilt/Windows/Scalars.lean) | A window reads a string or a list, sliced: never an integer or a Boolean. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Scope`](Stilt/Windows/Scope.lean) | Running a body with a variable bound, then ending the binding however the body ends (expressions.scope): both models end alike, the value the scope gives reading no source of the variable's name, and a value leaving the body none either, as the result's parameters no longer name it (views.escaping, E0422). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Sim`](Stilt/Windows/Sim.lean) | By induction on the fuel, every expression that the checks of views accept, run in a context whose scope keeps its bindings' sources, ends alike in both models, each form by its own case. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Steps`](Stilt/Windows/Steps.lean) | The simulation of the in-place model by the model of windows, and the steps of the evaluator that keep it. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Store`](Stilt/Windows/Store.lean) | How the changes of a store keep the windows that hold: a window reads its region, the part of its location at its path, and a change that leaves the region as it was in both stores keeps the window holding and every value whose windows it is read alike (`Holds.region`, `VRel.region`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Through`](Stilt/Windows/Through.lean) | A view's part is what its window reads (views.what-view-is). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Through.Maps`](Stilt/Windows/Through/Maps.lean) | What `mapWindows` gives of a list, a record and a variant, element by element: the same elements at the same indexes and names, each with its windows replaced, which writes at an index or a name keep; where the windows of an element, of a list's set element and of a record's set field are among a value's; and the test by which `windowAt` tells a value that holds no window, a map of every window to `stuck` (`mapWindows_stuck_isOk`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Through.Parts`](Stilt/Windows/Through/Parts.lean) | A window narrowed by a range reads its part sliced by that range (`partIn_append`, `part_append`; views.making-view). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Top`](Stilt/Windows/Top.lean) | `main` starts from empty stores, in a context with no variable, no read around it and no caller. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Unbind`](Stilt/Windows/Unbind.lean) | When the scope of a variable of the body ends, its location is freed. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.Values`](Stilt/Windows/Values.lean) | A variable, a literal, a function or a method, and a record, variant or list literal, on the relation of windows: both models end alike, with related values. |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.View`](Stilt/Windows/View.lean) | `View.of(source, …)` and `View.all(source)` on the relation of windows (views.making-view): the source evaluated in both models; in the model of windows, a window on the place it names when that takes no index (`viewOf`), the window the place holds, or the value read; that value waiting, its sources read, while the offsets run (views.reads-that-last); then narrowed as `View.of` slices the in-place value (`narrow`). | [views.making-view](../reference/13-modes.md#views.making-view) |
| [`Stilt.Windows.View.Narrow`](Stilt/Windows/View/Narrow.lean) | A value read along a path with no window met before its end reads alike through windows (`getW_of_get`), and holds only windows of the value (`windowsOf_get`). |  |
| [`Stilt.Windows.View.Place`](Stilt/Windows/View/Place.lean) | A view's source that is a place with no index (`fieldPath`) is a variable followed by fields: an instance of a generic body has the same one (`inst_eq_of_place`), and both models evaluate it, once they have the fuel, by reading the state alone, the in-place model the part of the variable's place at its fields (`eval_place`; views.making-view). |  |

## Theorems

`Stilt.soundness` proves that every run of a program that passes the checks below keeps each promise below.

<!-- theorems -->
| Check of `Program.Accepted` | What it asks |
| --- | --- |
| `wellTyped` | Typing, with mutability and the no-escape check. |
| `exclusive` | The law of exclusivity (modes.law, E0402 and E0404). |
| `heldNames` | The law of exclusivity with the names of one `when` whose values have the same type as one binding (shared.names-that-may-be-one-place, E0402 and E0404). |
| `viewsRead` | No source of a view modified while the view lives (views.change-while-viewed, E0423). |
| `viewsContained` | No view outlives its sources' scopes (views.escaping, E0422). |
| `oneShot` | No body may resume twice on a path: no `resume` in a loop, in a closure literal, after another in sequence, or in a branch after one that resumed (handlers.resume-at-most-once, E0514), and none outside an operation's body. |
| `locks` | No `when` whose block holds a site of `locks`: a `when`, or a use of a function that has the inferred fact, called or as a value, a closure literal's body included (shared.locks, E0430). |
| `panicLimits` | Each function and `main` limited by `without panic` keeps the limit (E0204). |
| `overflowLimits` | Each function and `main` limited by `without panic, overflow` keeps both (E0204). |
| `divLimits` | Each function and `main` limited by `without div` keeps the limit (E0204). |
| `projections` | The rules of projections: every path through a projection's body runs one `yield` to its end or a `return` and at most one to a raise or a panic (projections.once-on-every-path, E0424); a `yield` only in a projection's body (projections.yield, E0425); a projection's body raises nothing of its own (projections.cannot-raise, E0427); and a projection named only as the callee of a call where a place is (projections.not-a-value, E0426). |
| `allocLimits` | Each function and `main` limited by `without alloc` keeps the limit (E0204). |
| `copyLimits` | Each function and `main` limited by `without copy` keeps the limit (E0204). |
| `viewsWrite` | The rules of writable bindings: while one writes a source, nothing else reaches it (projections.reaching-written-source, E0429), and a view given to a `var` writes every source it has (projections.writable-bindings, E0428). No promise below rests on it: a writable binding is a call that takes its source `inout`, and the closure that is its scope reaches every other variable by copy, so its runs agree in every model whatever the scope reaches. It is the check under which that copy is the compiler's reach-through: a scope that reaches no source reads, by copy, what the compiler reads in place. |
| `demands` | The demands the calculus writes: no value that `Shared.new` takes is `fixed`, by its type or, for a closure literal or a binding of one, by what it captures (modes.demands and shared.tasks-and-modes, E0432). |

| Promise of `Program.Promises` | What every run keeps | From |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `typed` | Finishing with `()`, a raise of the type the row allows, a panic or out of fuel; never stuck (`TypeSoundness`). | `typeSoundness` |
| `exclusive` | No access breaks exclusivity: in place shows and writes what copies do (`InPlaceAgreement`). | `inPlaceAgreement` |
| `heldNames` | shared.names-that-may-be-one-place: the block of every `when`, with any of its names written as another of the same type, as the export writes it for handles that reach one value, obeys the law of exclusivity, when it binds none of the `when`'s names (`Program.HeldNames.aliased`). So the law that `exclusive` rests on holds of the block that runs, whichever handles are equal. | `Program.HeldNames.aliased` |
| `views` | No view reads a source that changed or is gone: with views as windows into their sources' storage, the run shows and writes what copies do (views.what-view-is). A writable binding is a call that takes its source `inout` (`Form.writes`): with windows a write through it writes the source's storage at once, and in copies its copy, which the end of its scope, however it ends, writes back (projections.what-view-writes). | `inPlaceAgreement`, `Windows.cases_of`, `Windows.declsChecked_of`, `Windows.main_distinctParams`, `Windows.shows_windows` |
| `modes` | The reuse the modes allow shows nothing and writes nothing else, up to the marks in a raised error (`ModesAgreement`). | `modesAgreement` |
| `cycles` | A cell on a cycle, in the world the run ends in, is one of a `Shared<U>` whose `U` can reach itself (`on_cycle_can_reach_itself`, cycles.can-form-cycle). | `Program.WellTyped.cycles` |
| `whenDiscipline` | The discipline of `when`, a property of the evaluator that holds of every evaluation, whether the program is accepted or not: no code but the `when` holding a cell reads or writes its value. From a state whose world holds a cell, every state an evaluation reaches holds it, in every rest of a performance resumed where it is held too, and every cell stays in its place (`eval_keepsHeld`); and however a `when`'s block ends, the `when` ends the same way with each name's value put back in its cell and the cell let go, every other cell and the frames as they were (`release_puts_back`, shared.when). The calculus runs one task at a time, so this says nothing of threads. | `Program.keepsHeld` |
| `tasks` | tasks.continuations-stay-in-task: a call of a function value of a task function's type, which takes no parameters and whose row holds `io` at most (tasks.task-functions), from any typed state, never ends as a performance: no continuation leaves the task (`continuations_stay`). | `continuations_stay`, `Program.WellTyped.typedDecls` |
| `parallel` | par.sequential-meaning and par.panics: calls of function values whose types have empty rows, on arguments of their parameter types, run at the same time from one typed frame, end as they do run one after another, from the first to the last, in every typed world (`parallel_eq_sequential`). | `parallel_eq_sequential`, `Program.WellTyped.typedDecls` |
| `oneShot` | handlers.resume-at-most-once: whatever the order of write-back, the body of an operation of a handler that takes control, called by a `with` on one-shot values from a one-shot state, performs at most one `resume` on each path, so it runs the rest of the block at most once; and `main` performs none (`Program.OneShot.clause`, `Program.OneShot.main`). | `Program.OneShot.clause`, `Program.OneShot.main` |
| `locks` | shared.locks and shared.when-order: whatever the order of write-back, the block of every `when` of the program, as written or instantiated, run from a frame whose function values are lock-free, holds no cell in any state it reaches that the world it started in didn't, and neither does the rest of a performance, from the world it is resumed in (`Program.Locks.block`). So a task inside a `when` takes no other value before it releases its own, the premise of `Shared.Deadlock.Step`. A call through a function value made by code that locks may still lock (facts.calls-through-function-values). | `Program.Locks.block` |
| `projections` | projections.once-on-every-path: the body of every projection, run with any fuel from where the list its function keeps the access's values in is empty, ends with one value kept, the access's, on every path to its end or to a `return`, and with at most one on every path to a raise or a performance, whose rest, resumed, again ends so; no path ends with a `break` or a `continue` (`Program.Projections.yields`). So the caller's access runs once, and the value the function ends with is there. | `Program.Projections.yields` |
| `unseen` | handlers.when-place-is-seen: a handler's clause, called on its operation's argument as a `with` calls it, from whatever frames the code it ends left, runs the same and gives them back, one outcome for every frame with that frame put back (`callAs_unseen`): the body reaches no place of the calls it ends, so whether one is written back before or after the body runs can't be observed. A property of the evaluator, whether the program is accepted or not. | `callAs_unseen` |
| `panics` | effects.without-panic: a call of a function limited by `without panic`, and a run whose `main` is, panics only with `overflow` or `deadlock` (`Program.PanicsKeptBy`). | `Program.PanicKeptBy.kept` |
| `overflows` | effects.without-overflow: a call of a function limited by `without panic, overflow`, and a run whose `main` is, panics only with `deadlock` (`Program.PanicsKeptBy`). | `Program.PanicKeptBy.kept` |
| `halts` | facts.without-div: a call of a function limited by `without div`, on arguments whose values at its non-escaping parameters are function values that halt, through which its body may call with no `div` of its own (facts.div), and a run whose `main` is limited, halts (`Program.HaltsKept`). | `Program.DivLimited.kept` |
| `allocs` | facts.without-alloc: a call of a function limited by `without alloc`, on arguments that allocate nothing when called, and a run whose `main` is limited, run in the counting evaluator as the reference interpreter runs them, with no allocation counted (`Program.AllocsKept`). | `Program.AllocLimited.kept` |
| `quietCalls` | facts.io-and-capabilities: a call of a function value whose type's row reads as no `io`, on arguments of its parameter types, from any typed state, keeps the world's output, standard error and input, and so does the rest of each performance it makes, resumed in a typed world with a value of the type its operation gives, from that world (`call_keeps`). | `call_keeps`, `Decls.Typed.quiet`, `Program.WellTyped.typedDecls` |
| `quiet` | facts.io-and-capabilities: a run whose `main`'s row holds no `io` ends with nothing written to its output or its standard error, and its whole input unread (`Program.WellTyped.quiet`). | `Program.WellTyped.quiet` |
<!-- /theorems -->

## Pass-through effects
